Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on magnetics structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 985. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 213 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to magnetics theory, we decompose the problem into 985 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 213. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in magnetics theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 684 and the invariant I(A,B) = 403 mod 2.

For the inductive step, we decompose the magnetics structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 403. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 221.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Let the magnetics structure S satisfy the conditions of invariance principle framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 290. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 680 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).
